<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content>
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Civil War Presents</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>HOMEGROWN ALLDAYER 2026</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Featuring…</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">SERGEANT THUNDERHOOF</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">THE KRYSS TAIMETH EXPERIENCE</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">ERRONAUT</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">TEIGER</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">WOLVES IN WINTER</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BOREHEAD</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">THE RHUBARB</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">SUPERSEED</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">——————————</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Civil War are back with their THIRD annual Homegrown Alldayer on 12 September 2026 at the legendary Black Heart in Camden Town, London, with some of the very finest 'homegrown' stoner rock &amp; heavy psych vibes from across the UK.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This year's event features and exceptional line up with Sergeant Thunderhoof (Bristol), The Kryss Talmeth Experience (Edinburgh) , Erronaut (Home Counties), Teiger (London), Wolves in Winter (Bradford), The Rhubarb (Glasgow), Borehead (London) and Superseed (Bristol) representing the very best of the U.K. heavy scene, during what might well be the classic era for U.K. stoner. All in all it is going to be - without a doubt - an event to remember, and not one to be missed!</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content>
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>The Black Heart in assoc with ITB presents…</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>TOSELAND</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Following on from the announcement of their November slot at Winterstorm Festival, Toseland have confirmed their first club shows since 2018. They are set to play 3 UK shows in September, deliberately selecting tiny venues to create an intimate atmosphere for their fans, in Leeds, Cannock and London.&nbsp;</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Toseland have also announced that they will headline the live music event at the British MotoGP, on the main stage at Silverstone on Saturday 8th August. This is sure to be a special homecoming for frontman and double World Superbikes Champion James Toseland, a stage he last performed on in 2013 to a rapturous response.&nbsp;</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Its been 8 years since the classic songs from their 2 critically acclaimed albums Renegade in 2013 and Cradle The Rage in 2016, have been played on the stages of the UK. Back then they toured regularly across the UK and Europe, with headline shows in increasingly large venues punctuated by support tours with the likes of Deep Purple, Black Stone Cherry, Skid Row and Theory.&nbsp;</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Its been a long road back for James, his work with the band curtailed in 2018 by operations on the severely damaged right wrist that ended his racing career and the long period of recovery that followed meaning that he could not play the piano that is so vital to the band’s sound. In that time his TV career took over, and he can now be seen and heard as pundit and commentator for World Superbikes on TNT and HBO Max, travelling the world providing expert insight.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>2024 saw James perform live acoustically on the An Evening With James Toseland tour, where new songs were aired and stories were shared. The response to these intimate events gave James the desire to get Toseland out and about again. The hunt for band members commenced, and great players and people were found.&nbsp;</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>So here we are in 2026. Plans are afoot for new music, watch this space for news on that, but for for now prepare yourselves for the whirlwind live experience that Toseland provide.&nbsp;</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>James Toseland – I am so happy to announce that I am going to be performing with the band again. Its been a long journey with multiple operations on my wrist, but I am now able to play again and can’t wait for the first show’.</em></p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Gotobeat x Siren Concerts present...</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>AGABAS</strong><br><em>Plus guests</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>APATHY</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Norwegian deathjazz six-piece Agabas are returning to the UK for the second time (hi, it’s us, we are writing this ourselves), but they are not the same. Since their last run in the UK just a year ago, the band has been on a European tour supporting Avatar, signed a big record deal with Mascot Records, and most importantly, written some sweet sweet new material for you.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Let’s be honest guys, last time was fun. But this time we are hungrier, and we’re going to take it out on you. Don’t bring your loved ones if you want to keep them around (just kidding it’s actually going to be a lot of fun, you should totally bring them). Writing a bio is hard, just come to the show, it’ll be sick ok see you!</em></p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content>
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>CHAIDURA<br>[LIMINAL BEYOND]</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Plus special guest…</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>SHIROBON</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>———</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>THE SHOW</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>LIMINAL BEYOND is the final, grand-scale chapter of Chaidura’s Liminal era.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Following the release of the final track this September, the Liminal EP is officially complete. Where LIMINAL BEGINNING introduced you to the threshold, LIMINAL BEYOND pushes past it—symbolising growth, reflection, and the evolution into our next phase.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Known for a sound that blends the theatricality of Japanese Visual Kei with the heavy, intricate energy of Emo and Alternative Metal, Chaidura creates a "cinematic chaos" on stage. For this special final show of 2026, Chaidura will perform the completed EP, songs omitted from the summer tour, and exclusive new material.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong><em>THE VENUE</em></strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>We are bringing this experience to The Black Heart, an iconic pillar of Camden Town’s alternative and heavy music scene. Tucked away in the heart of London's underground, its high-energy gig room provides the intimate, raw, and unrelenting atmosphere needed to close out this chapter in full force.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong><em>TICKET TIERS</em></strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Early Bird Double Ticket (£25): 2 x General Admission tickets (Available until 31st August)</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Single General Admission (£15): 1 x Standard entry</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong><em>GOOD TO KNOW</em></strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Date: Saturday, 21st November 2026 Doors: 7:00 PM Opener: 7:30 PM (TBD) Chaidura Start: 8:15 PM Meet &amp; Greet: 9:45 PM Age Restriction: 18+ Only Location: 2-3 Greenland Place, Camden Town, London, NW1 0AP ID: Please bring a valid photo ID for entry and bar service. Merch: Brand-new, show-exclusive merch will be available at the venue.</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Grabbed your tickets? Tag @chaidura_ on IG/TikTok so I can see who’s joining us for LIMINAL BEYOND.</em></p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content>
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Beyond The Grave present </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>EPICARDIECTOMY (CZ)</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>EMBRYECTOMY (GRE)</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>INVERECUND (ITA)</strong></p><p style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;" class=""><strong>Epicardiectomy</strong></p><p style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;" class=""><em>Epicardiectomy takes its history back in the year of 2007 and needs no introduction to the fans of the genre. Pioneers of Czech slamming brutal death metal have released 3 full-length albums and 1 split CD album during the band‘s existence and played hundreds of shows and festivals as well as numerous tours all over the world including Europe, North America, South America and Japan, and have grown into one of the most recognized bands in the modern brutal death metal/slam scene. The band is currently working on their 4th full length album which is promising to be their most uncompromising and brutal to date.&nbsp;</em></p><p style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;" class=""><strong>Embryectomy</strong></p><p style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;" class=""><em>They were formed in 2013 and released their debut Promo on 2014. In 2015 the split “Realm of Endless Massacre” was released along with Traumatomy and Cerebral Paralysis. In 2016, the band released their first full length album “Gluttonous Mastication of Embryonic Remnants” via Morbid Generation Records. This year, they are gonna release their second split collaboration, together with Psychosomatic Self-Mutilation and Nephrectomy via Amputated Vein Records, called “Absolution through Sacred Extrication”. In 2020 the band released the second full length album "Flamethrower Ecdysis".</em></p><p style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;" class=""><strong>Inverecund</strong></p><p style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;" class=""><em>Formed in 2019 by Marcello Olivi and Morgan Bernadini, the band offers fast and Brutal Death Metal inspired by the likes of Gorgasm and Inveracity. Subsequently, Michele Sassano is added to the formation and they record their first Ep “Engrossed to Horripilation” released for New Standard Elite in 2023.</em></p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content>
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>BLATOIDEA | BAD NASTY | DEFCON ZERO | DOWN CHARMED</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Sat 06.06.2026 — The Black Heart, London</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">In memory of our beloved Jacko.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This is not just another gig. this is a night with a mission.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">On June 6th, 2026, we’re gathering at The Black Heart to celebrate the life and spirit of our late bass player Jacko, whose strength, humour, and heart inspired all of us.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Jacko battled myositis, a rare and debilitating condition, with courage that deserves to be remembered and fought against.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">We’re raising funds for Myositis UK, an organization doing vital work to support people living with this disease and drive research toward better treatments.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Every ticket, every pint, every moment you share with us that night helps push that mission forward.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This is our way of keeping Jacko’s memory alive, turning grief into purpose, passion, and community.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Line-up:</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>BLATOIDEA<br>Bad Nasty<br>Defcon Zero<br>Down Charmed</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">These bands are here because they’re family, they believe in what we’re doing, and they want to make this night powerful and unforgettable.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This event is for everyone who has ever lost someone, who has ever stood by a friend in a struggle, who believes music can do more than entertain. It can heal, remember, and change lives.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Come down. Bring friends.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Make a difference.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">All proceeds go to Myositis UK</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">https://www.myositis.org.uk/</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content>
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>THE NEEDFUL THINGS</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Plus guests</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>MATTED</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>The Needful Things are an 'underground supergroup' features members of  - The Wildhearts, The Spangles,  New Generation Superstars, Teenage Casket Company, Love/Hate, Jetboy, Drugdealer Cheerleader, Waterbratz!, and Enuff Z’nuff.  The band will be smashing out an hour of material from all of these bands and more!</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>Support will come from Matted.  Matted are one of the UK’s most exciting new rock bands, blending heavy riffs with emotional depth and sonic experimentation. Since their debut in May 2024, the five-piece has built fast momentum—playing Download Festival, Call of the Wild, Planet Rockstock, and supporting Crashface, Armored Saint, and Von Hertzen Brothers, alongside sold-out headline shows in London and Brighton. They were finalists in Kerrang! Radio’s The Deal. and performed at Download festival District X in 2025.</em></p>
